Use to turn edited partition trees into a working, flashed ROM. ([jawadaboumehsen/LtiRom](https://github.com/jawadaboumehsen/LtiRom/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/customrom-repack-flash) · 2 copies)
- **customs**: Fetch Korea Customs Service (관세청) monthly item trade for one HS code from data.go.kr -- exports, imports, and weight by year-month. Holds no logic of its own -- it calls the pydatagokr package's CLI (`datagokr customs item_trade`) and shows the result to the user. Trigger phrases: 수출입실적, 품목별 수출입, HS 코드 수출, 관세청 무역통계, customs trade, item trade, exports by HS code.
